CULLAVAGGAM NITTHITAM. 


VARIOUS READINGS AND EXTRACTS FROM THE 
COMMENTARY OF BUDDHAGHOSA. 


A: India Office MS. of the Phayre Collection (Burmese 
writing). About half of the work (from VI. 4. 8 to the 
end) had been lost in this MS. when I collated it. The 
missing part has since been recovered, but I have not seen it. 

B: MS. of the Paris National Library, fonds Påli 20 
(Binhalese writing). 

C: MS. of the Royal Library at Berlin, Orient. fol. 949 
(Burmese writing). 

D: Buppmaauosa's Atthakatbå ; MS. of the Berlin Royal 
Library, Orient. fol. 931 (Burmese writing).

duced in the Cullavagga, aa in the whole Pitaka, by myself.
